	and t2.DtEntradaProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim
	order by 1

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;

   CREATE TABLE Central_TbFunilSimul_TEP AS
   SELECT

	Case
		when t1.cdparceirocomercial in (418254, 378215) then "MFS"
    	Else "CS"
	End as CanalComercial,

	Case
		when t1.TpPessoa = "F" and t1.DtSimulacaoProposta > '13Jun2025'd and (input(SUBSTR(t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu,6,2),18.) between 00 and 34) then "Delta 1"
		when t1.TpPessoa = "F" and t1.DtSimulacaoProposta > '13Jun2025'd and (input(SUBSTR(t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu,6,2),18.) between 35 and 64) then "Delta 2"
		when t1.TpPessoa = "F" and t1.DtSimulacaoProposta > '13Jun2025'd and (input(SUBSTR(t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu,6,2),18.) between 65 and 79) then "Desafiante 1"
		when t1.TpPessoa = "F" and t1.DtSimulacaoProposta > '13Jun2025'd and (input(SUBSTR(t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu,6,2),18.) between 80 and 89) then "Desafiante 2"
		when t1.TpPessoa = "F" and t1.DtSimulacaoProposta > '13Jun2025'd and (input(SUBSTR(t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu,6,2),18.) between 90 and 99) then "Grupo Controle"
	End as GrupoDeltas,

	Case
	    when t1.GhEntrada in (0, 2.5, 3, 3.5, 4.5, 4.75, 5.25) then 5.25
	    when t1.GhEntrada = 5.5 then 5.50
	    when t1.GhEntrada in (6.25, 6.5) then 6.50
	    when t1.GhEntrada = 7 then 7.00
	    when t1.GhEntrada = 7.5 then 7.50
	    when t1.GhEntrada in (7.75, 8) then 8.00
	    when t1.GhEntrada in (8.25, 8.5, 9) then 8.50
	End as NuScoreFimAj,

	COUNT (DISTINCT t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu) AS QtdSimu,
	COUNT (Distinct MSGPOS.NuCpfCNPJSimu) AS QtdSimuAprov,
	SUM (t1.FlPropostaOriginada) AS QtdOrig,
	SUM (t1.FlPropostaAprovada) AS QtdAprov,
	SUM (case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then 1 else 0 end) AS QtdEfet,

	SUM (t1.VrPropostaOriginada) AS VrOrig,
	SUM (t1.VrPropostaAprovada) AS VrAprov,
	SUM (case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end) AS VrEfet,



	(COUNT (Distinct MSGPOS.NuCpfCNPJSimu) / COUNT (DISTINCT t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu)) as MSgPositiva format=percent8.2,
	(SUM (t1.FlPropostaOriginada) / COUNT (Distinct MSGPOS.NuCpfCNPJSimu)) as Proposta format=percent8.2,
	(SUM (t1.FlPropostaAprovada) / SUM (t1.FlPropostaOriginada)) as Aprovacao format=percent8.2,
	(SUM (case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then 1 else 0 end) / SUM (t1.FlPropostaAprovada)) as Efetivacao format=percent8.2,

	(SUM (case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then 1 else 0 end) / COUNT (DISTINCT t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu)) as ConvGeral format=percent8.2,
	((COUNT (Distinct MSGPOS.NuCpfCNPJSimu) / COUNT (DISTINCT t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu)) * (SUM (t1.FlPropostaAprovada) / SUM (t1.FlPropostaOriginada))) as FatorAprovacao format=percent8.2,
	((SUM (t1.FlPropostaOriginada) / COUNT (Distinct MSGPOS.NuCpfCNPJSimu)) * (SUM (case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then 1 else 0 end) / SUM (t1.FlPropostaAprovada))) as FatorEfetivacao format=percent8.2,



	
	(SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Orig)) AS Valor_Tx_Orig,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Orig)) AS Valor_Tx_Pactuada_Orig,
	(SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Orig)) AS Valor_TIR_Orig,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada)) AS Tx_Orig,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada)) AS Tx_Pactuada_Ori,
	((1+SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada))/(1+SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada))-1) AS Spread_Net_Orig,
	((1+SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada))/(1+SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada))-1) AS Spread_Pactuada_Orig,

	
	(SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Aprov)) AS Valor_Tx_Aprov,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Aprov)) AS Valor_Tx_Pactuada_Aprov,
	(SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Aprov)) AS Valor_TIR_Aprov,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada)) AS Tx_Aprov,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada)) AS Tx_Pactuada_Aprov,
	((1+SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada))/(1+SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada))-1) AS Spread_Net_Aprov,
	((1+SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada))/(1+SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada))-1) AS Spread_Pactuada_Aprov,

	
	(SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxNet_Efet else 0 end)) AS Valor_Tx_Efet,
	(SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxPactuada_Efet else 0 end)) AS Valor_Tx_Pactuada_Efet,
	(SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTirContabil_Efet else 0 end)) AS Valor_TIR_Efet,
	(SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxNet_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end)) AS Tx_Efet,
	(SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxPactuada_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end)) AS Tx_Pactuada_Efet,
	((1+SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxNet_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end))/(1+SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTirContabil_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end))-1) AS Spread_Net_Efet,
	((1+SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxPactuada_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end))/(1+SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTirContabil_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end))-1) AS Spread_Pactuada_Efet,


	SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then VpPrazo_Efet else 0 end) as VpPrazo_Efet,
	SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then VpPrazo_Efet else 0 end) / SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada  else 0 end) as PrazoEfet,
	SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end) / SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then 1 else 0 end) as TicketEfet,
	SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VrPDD else 0 end) as VrPerdaEstimada

	FROM Work.TbFunilSimul_Filtros as t1

	Left Join
	   (Select CodSimu, NuCpfCNPJSimu
	    From Bases.TbFunilSimulacao_Solar
	    Where DtSimulacaoProposta between &Data_Simul and &Data_Simul_Fim and FlMSGPositiva = 1
	    Group by 1) as MSGPOS
	On t1.CodSimu = MSGPOS.CodSimu

	Left Join WORK.base_solar_originacao as t4 on (t1.NuProposta = t4.NuProposta)

	GROUP BY 1,2,3
	order by 1

;QUIT;

proc sql;

Create table base_solar as
Select distinct
t1.*,

(t1.DtPrimeiroVencimento - t1.DtContrato)*(t1.VrProducao) as VrCarencia,

	Case
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null) then a.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null) then b.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null) then c.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null) then d.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		else t1.VpTxGerencialRebate
	End as VpTxNet,

	Case
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null) then a.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null) then b.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null) then c.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null) then d.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		else t1.VpTxJuros
	End as VpTxPactuada,


Case
	when t3.NmCampanhaCupom = "" THEN "-"
	else t3.NmCampanhaCupom
End as NmCampanhaCupom,

Case
	when t3.NmCampanhaCupom = "" then "N"
	else "S"
End as Flag_Cupom,

	Case
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVXGK','BV7YG') then 'CS_Padrão'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV13J','BV7YH') then 'CS_Top 1'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV4IT','BV7YI') then 'CS_Top 2'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVXGQ','BV7Y1') then 'MFS_Padrão'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVYB3') then 'MFS_Combate I'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVYB4') then 'MFS_Combate II'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV29Y','BV7YJ') then 'MFS_Top'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVXVD','BV7YW') then 'MFS_Contingencia'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7YZ','BVXGO') then 'Comissionamento 1'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7YY','BVXGP') then 'Comissionamento 2'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7II') then 'Feirao'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7LV','BV7YX') then 'Guerrilha Padrão'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV60U', 'BV7DH', 'BV7LH') then 'Belenus'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7LX') then 'Fotus'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7U5') then 'ASC'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7O3') then 'SolarPrime'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV654') then 'AVT'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7FL') then 'EcoPower'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7YB') then 'Guerrilha Sul'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV2B5','BV653','BV6L3') then 'Especiais'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in  ('BV600', 'BV679', 'BV670', 'BV68A', 'BV68B', 'BV68C', 'BV7D5', 'BV7EX', 'BV50M', 'BV50L', 'BV6WD', 'BV6WF', 'BV6WH', 'BV6WE', 'BV7DD', 'BV6WG', 'BV7M2', 'BV7L5', 'BV7L4', 'BV71F', 'BV7M3') then 'Rebate'
	 	else 'Outras'
	 End as DsTipoTabela,

	Case
		when t1.TpPessoa = "F" and t1.DtEntradaProposta between '14Jun2025'd and '05Sep2025'd and (input(SUBSTR(t1.NuCpfCnpj,6,2),18.) between 00 and 34) then "Delta 1"
		when t1.TpPessoa = "F" and t1.DtEntradaProposta between '14Jun2025'd and '05Sep2025'd and (input(SUBSTR(t1.NuCpfCnpj,6,2),18.) between 35 and 64) then "Delta 2"
		when t1.TpPessoa = "F" and t1.DtEntradaProposta between '14Jun2025'd and '05Sep2025'd and (input(SUBSTR(t1.NuCpfCnpj,6,2),18.) between 65 and 79) then "Desafiante 1"
		when t1.TpPessoa = "F" and t1.DtEntradaProposta between '14Jun2025'd and '05Sep2025'd and (input(SUBSTR(t1.NuCpfCnpj,6,2),18.) between 80 and 89) then "Desafiante 2"
		when t1.TpPessoa = "F" and t1.DtEntradaProposta between '14Jun2025'd and '05Sep2025'd and (input(SUBSTR(t1.NuCpfCnpj,6,2),18.) between 90 and 99) then "Grupo Controle"
	End as GrupoDeltas,

	Case
		when t1.NuScoreFim between 0.00 and 5.25 then 5.25
		when t1.NuScoreFim between 5.26 and 5.50 then 5.50
		when t1.NuScoreFim between 5.51 and 6.50 then 6.50
		when t1.NuScoreFim between 6.51 and 7.00 then 7.00
		when t1.NuScoreFim between 7.01 and 7.50 then 7.50
		when t1.NuScoreFim between 7.51 and 8.00 then 8.00
		when t1.NuScoreFim >= 8.01 then 8.50
	End as NuScoreFimAj,

	Case
		when t1.cdparceirocomercial in (418254, 378215) then "MFS"
    	Else "CS"
	End as CanalComercial

from HOML.tbproducao as t1
left join AUXL.tbinfocupom as t3 on (t3.NuProposta = t1.NuProposta)
inner join WORK.base_solar_originacao as t4 on (t1.NuProposta = t4.NuProposta)

left join tuyra.solar_rebate_0424 as a on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=a.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=a.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_0624 as b on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=b.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=b.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_1024 as c on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=c.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=c.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_&Data_Rebate as d on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=d.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=d.PRAZO)

where t1.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('Solar') and t4.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim

;quit;



PROC SORT DATA=base_solar NODUPKEY;
    BY NuProposta;
RUN;


proc sql;

	create table dados_medios as

		select DISTINCT
			a.nmgrupoprodutometa as Produto
			,a.CanalComercial
			,a.GrupoDeltas
			,NuScoreFimAj

			,sum(a.VrProducao) as Producao
			,sum(a.VrProducao)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as Ticket
			,sum(a.VrComissaoTotal+a.VrComissaoParcelada)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcComissao

			,sum(a.VrPerdaEstimada)/sum(a.VrFinanciamento) as RUPE

			,sum(a.VrTarifaCadastro)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrTc
			,sum(a.VpTxNet)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTxNet
			,sum(a.VpTxPactuada)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTxPac
			,sum(a.VpTirContabil)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTir
			,round(sum(a.VpPrazo)/sum(a.VrProducao),1) as Prazo
			,sum(a.VrSeguros)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrSeguros
			,sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as QntContratos
			,round(sum(a.VrCarencia)/sum(a.VrProducao),1) as CARENCIA

		from work.base_solar as a

	group by 1,2,3,4

;quit;

%LET Data_Simul = '06Sep2025'd;
%LET Data_Simul_Fim = '06Oct2025'd;
%LET Data_Rebate = 0925;

PROC SQL;

   CREATE TABLE TbFunilSimul_Lista_Propostas AS
   SELECT

	t1.NuAnoMesSimu,
	t1.NuProposta

	FROM Bases.TBFUNILSIMULACAO_SOLAR as t1
	left join HOML.TBORIGINACAO as t2 on (t1.NuProposta=t2.NuProposta)

	WHERE t1.DtSimulacaoProposta between &Data_Simul and &Data_Simul_Fim and t1.NuProposta is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada = 1
	and t2.DtEntradaProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim
	order by 1

;QUIT;





proc sql;

Create table base_solar_originacao as
Select distinct
t1.*,

	

Case
    
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then a.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then b.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then c.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then d.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
	when (t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then t1.VpTxGerencialRebate
    else .
End as VpTxNet_Orig,

Case
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then a.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then b.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then c.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then d.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then t1.VpTxPactuada
    else .
End as VpTxPactuada_Orig,

Case
    when (t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then t1.VpTirContabil
    else .
End as VpTirContabil_Orig,



Case
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then a.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then b.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then c.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then d.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then t1.VpTxGerencialRebate
    else .
End as VpTxNet_Aprov,

Case
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then a.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then b.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then c.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then d.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then t1.VpTxPactuada
    else .
End as VpTxPactuada_Aprov,

Case
    when (t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then t1.VpTirContabil
    else .
End as VpTirContabil_Aprov,



Case
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then a.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then b.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then c.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then d.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then t1.VpTxGerencialRebate
    else .
End as VpTxNet_Efet,

Case
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then a.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then b.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then c.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then d.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then t1.VpTxPactuada
    else .
End as VpTxPactuada_Efet,

Case
    when (t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then t1.VpTirContabil
    else .
End as VpTirContabil_Efet,


Case
	when t3.NmCampanhaCupom = "" THEN "-"
	else t3.NmCampanhaCupom
End as NmCampanhaCupom,

Case
	when t3.NmCampanhaCupom = "" then "N"
	else "S"
End as Flag_Cupom,



Case
    when (t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then t1.VpPrazo
    else .
End as VpPrazo_Efet

from HOML.TBORIGINACAO as t1
inner join Work.TbFunilSimul_Lista_Propostas as t2 on (t2.NuProposta = t1.NuProposta)
left join AUXL.tbinfocupom as t3 on (t3.NuProposta = t1.NuProposta)

left join tuyra.solar_rebate_0424 as a on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=a.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=a.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_0624 as b on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=b.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=b.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_1024 as c on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=c.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=c.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_&Data_Rebate as d on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=d.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=d.PRAZO)

where t1.DtEntradaProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim

;quit;



PROC SORT DATA=base_solar_originacao NODUPKEY;
    BY NuProposta;
RUN;





PROC SQL;

   CREATE TABLE TbFunilSimul_Filtros AS
   SELECT

	t1.*,
	t2.DtEntradaProposta,
	t2.DtStatusProposta

	FROM Bases.TBFUNILSIMULACAO_SOLAR as t1
	left join HOML.TBORIGINACAO as t2 on (t1.NuProposta=t2.NuProposta)

	WHERE t1.DtSimulacaoProposta between &Data_Simul and &Data_Simul_Fim
	and t2.DtEntradaProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim
	order by 1

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;

   CREATE TABLE Central_TbFunilSimul_SemCupom AS
   SELECT

	t1.TpPessoa,

	COUNT (DISTINCT t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu) AS QtdSimu,
	COUNT (Distinct MSGPOS.NuCpfCNPJSimu) AS QtdSimuAprov,
	SUM (t1.FlPropostaOriginada) AS QtdOrig,
	SUM (t1.FlPropostaAprovada) AS QtdAprov,
	SUM (case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then 1 else 0 end) AS QtdEfet,

	SUM (t1.VrPropostaOriginada) AS VrOrig,
	SUM (t1.VrPropostaAprovada) AS VrAprov,
	SUM (case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end) AS VrEfet,



	(COUNT (Distinct MSGPOS.NuCpfCNPJSimu) / COUNT (DISTINCT t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu)) as MSgPositiva format=percent8.2,
	(SUM (t1.FlPropostaOriginada) / COUNT (Distinct MSGPOS.NuCpfCNPJSimu)) as Proposta format=percent8.2,
	(SUM (t1.FlPropostaAprovada) / SUM (t1.FlPropostaOriginada)) as Aprovacao format=percent8.2,
	(SUM (case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then 1 else 0 end) / SUM (t1.FlPropostaAprovada)) as Efetivacao format=percent8.2,

	(SUM (case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then 1 else 0 end) / COUNT (DISTINCT t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu)) as ConvGeral format=percent8.2,
	((COUNT (Distinct MSGPOS.NuCpfCNPJSimu) / COUNT (DISTINCT t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu)) * (SUM (t1.FlPropostaAprovada) / SUM (t1.FlPropostaOriginada))) as FatorAprovacao format=percent8.2,
	((SUM (t1.FlPropostaOriginada) / COUNT (Distinct MSGPOS.NuCpfCNPJSimu)) * (SUM (case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then 1 else 0 end) / SUM (t1.FlPropostaAprovada))) as FatorEfetivacao format=percent8.2,



	
	(SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Orig)) AS Valor_Tx_Orig,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Orig)) AS Valor_Tx_Pactuada_Orig,
	(SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Orig)) AS Valor_TIR_Orig,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada)) AS Tx_Orig,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada)) AS Tx_Pactuada_Ori,
	((1+SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada))/(1+SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada))-1) AS Spread_Net_Orig,
	((1+SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada))/(1+SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada))-1) AS Spread_Pactuada_Orig,

	
	(SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Aprov)) AS Valor_Tx_Aprov,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Aprov)) AS Valor_Tx_Pactuada_Aprov,
	(SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Aprov)) AS Valor_TIR_Aprov,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada)) AS Tx_Aprov,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada)) AS Tx_Pactuada_Aprov,
	((1+SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada))/(1+SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada))-1) AS Spread_Net_Aprov,
	((1+SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada))/(1+SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada))-1) AS Spread_Pactuada_Aprov,

	
	(SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxNet_Efet else 0 end)) AS Valor_Tx_Efet,
	(SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxPactuada_Efet else 0 end)) AS Valor_Tx_Pactuada_Efet,
	(SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTirContabil_Efet else 0 end)) AS Valor_TIR_Efet,
	(SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxNet_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end)) AS Tx_Efet,
	(SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxPactuada_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end)) AS Tx_Pactuada_Efet,
	((1+SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxNet_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end))/(1+SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTirContabil_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end))-1) AS Spread_Net_Efet,
	((1+SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxPactuada_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end))/(1+SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTirContabil_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end))-1) AS Spread_Pactuada_Efet,


	SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then VpPrazo_Efet else 0 end) as VpPrazo_Efet,
	SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then VpPrazo_Efet else 0 end) / SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada  else 0 end) as PrazoEfet,
	SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end) / SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then 1 else 0 end) as TicketEfet,
	SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VrPDD else 0 end) as VrPerdaEstimada

	FROM Work.TbFunilSimul_Filtros as t1

	Left Join
	   (Select CodSimu, NuCpfCNPJSimu
	    From Work.TbFunilSimul_Filtros
	    Where DtSimulacaoProposta between &Data_Simul and &Data_Simul_Fim and FlMSGPositiva = 1
	    Group by 1) as MSGPOS
	On t1.CodSimu = MSGPOS.CodSimu

	Left Join WORK.base_solar_originacao as t4 on (t1.NuProposta = t4.NuProposta)

	GROUP BY 1
	order by 1

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;

   CREATE TABLE Central_TbFunilSimul_Cupom AS
   SELECT

	t1.TpPessoa,

	COUNT (DISTINCT t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu) AS QtdSimu,
	COUNT (Distinct MSGPOS.NuCpfCNPJSimu) AS QtdSimuAprov,
	SUM (t1.FlPropostaOriginada) AS QtdOrig,
	SUM (t1.FlPropostaAprovada) AS QtdAprov,
	SUM (case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then 1 else 0 end) AS QtdEfet,

	SUM (t1.VrPropostaOriginada) AS VrOrig,
	SUM (t1.VrPropostaAprovada) AS VrAprov,
	SUM (case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end) AS VrEfet,



	(COUNT (Distinct MSGPOS.NuCpfCNPJSimu) / COUNT (DISTINCT t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu)) as MSgPositiva format=percent8.2,
	(SUM (t1.FlPropostaOriginada) / COUNT (Distinct MSGPOS.NuCpfCNPJSimu)) as Proposta format=percent8.2,
	(SUM (t1.FlPropostaAprovada) / SUM (t1.FlPropostaOriginada)) as Aprovacao format=percent8.2,
	(SUM (case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then 1 else 0 end) / SUM (t1.FlPropostaAprovada)) as Efetivacao format=percent8.2,

	(SUM (case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then 1 else 0 end) / COUNT (DISTINCT t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu)) as ConvGeral format=percent8.2,
	((COUNT (Distinct MSGPOS.NuCpfCNPJSimu) / COUNT (DISTINCT t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu)) * (SUM (t1.FlPropostaAprovada) / SUM (t1.FlPropostaOriginada))) as FatorAprovacao format=percent8.2,
	((SUM (t1.FlPropostaOriginada) / COUNT (Distinct MSGPOS.NuCpfCNPJSimu)) * (SUM (case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then 1 else 0 end) / SUM (t1.FlPropostaAprovada))) as FatorEfetivacao format=percent8.2,



	
	(SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Orig)) AS Valor_Tx_Orig,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Orig)) AS Valor_Tx_Pactuada_Orig,
	(SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Orig)) AS Valor_TIR_Orig,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada)) AS Tx_Orig,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada)) AS Tx_Pactuada_Ori,
	((1+SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada))/(1+SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada))-1) AS Spread_Net_Orig,
	((1+SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada))/(1+SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada))-1) AS Spread_Pactuada_Orig,

	
	(SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Aprov)) AS Valor_Tx_Aprov,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Aprov)) AS Valor_Tx_Pactuada_Aprov,
	(SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Aprov)) AS Valor_TIR_Aprov,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada)) AS Tx_Aprov,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada)) AS Tx_Pactuada_Aprov,
	((1+SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada))/(1+SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada))-1) AS Spread_Net_Aprov,
	((1+SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada))/(1+SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada))-1) AS Spread_Pactuada_Aprov,

	
	(SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxNet_Efet else 0 end)) AS Valor_Tx_Efet,
	(SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxPactuada_Efet else 0 end)) AS Valor_Tx_Pactuada_Efet,
	(SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTirContabil_Efet else 0 end)) AS Valor_TIR_Efet,
	(SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxNet_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end)) AS Tx_Efet,
	(SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxPactuada_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end)) AS Tx_Pactuada_Efet,
	((1+SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxNet_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end))/(1+SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTirContabil_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end))-1) AS Spread_Net_Efet,
	((1+SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxPactuada_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end))/(1+SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTirContabil_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end))-1) AS Spread_Pactuada_Efet,


	SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then VpPrazo_Efet else 0 end) as VpPrazo_Efet,
	SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then VpPrazo_Efet else 0 end) / SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada  else 0 end) as PrazoEfet,
	SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end) / SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then 1 else 0 end) as TicketEfet,
	SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VrPDD else 0 end) as VrPerdaEstimada

	FROM Work.TbFunilSimul_Filtros as t1

	Left Join
	   (Select CodSimu, NuCpfCNPJSimu
	    From Bases.TbFunilSimulacao_Solar
	    Where DtSimulacaoProposta between &Data_Simul and &Data_Simul_Fim and FlMSGPositiva = 1
	    Group by 1) as MSGPOS
	On t1.CodSimu = MSGPOS.CodSimu

	Left Join WORK.base_solar_originacao as t4 on (t1.NuProposta = t4.NuProposta)

	Where t4.Flag_Cupom = 'S'

	GROUP BY 1
	order by 1

;QUIT;

proc sql;

Create table base_solar as
Select distinct
t1.*,

(t1.DtPrimeiroVencimento - t1.DtContrato)*(t1.VrProducao) as VrCarencia,

	Case
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null) then a.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null) then b.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null) then c.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null) then d.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		else t1.VpTxGerencialRebate
	End as VpTxNet,

	Case
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null) then a.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null) then b.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null) then c.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null) then d.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		else t1.VpTxJuros
	End as VpTxPactuada,


Case
	when t3.NmCampanhaCupom = "" THEN "-"
	else t3.NmCampanhaCupom
End as NmCampanhaCupom,

Case
	when t3.NmCampanhaCupom = "" then "N"
	else "S"
End as Flag_Cupom,

	Case
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVXGK','BV7YG') then 'CS_Padrão'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV13J','BV7YH') then 'CS_Top 1'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV4IT','BV7YI') then 'CS_Top 2'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVXGQ','BV7Y1') then 'MFS_Padrão'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVYB3') then 'MFS_Combate I'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVYB4') then 'MFS_Combate II'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV29Y','BV7YJ') then 'MFS_Top'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVXVD','BV7YW') then 'MFS_Contingencia'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7YZ','BVXGO') then 'Comissionamento 1'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7YY','BVXGP') then 'Comissionamento 2'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7II') then 'Feirao'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7LV','BV7YX') then 'Guerrilha Padrão'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV60U', 'BV7DH', 'BV7LH') then 'Belenus'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7LX') then 'Fotus'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7U5') then 'ASC'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7O3') then 'SolarPrime'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV654') then 'AVT'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7FL') then 'EcoPower'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7YB') then 'Guerrilha Sul'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV2B5','BV653','BV6L3') then 'Especiais'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in  ('BV600', 'BV679', 'BV670', 'BV68A', 'BV68B', 'BV68C', 'BV7D5', 'BV7EX', 'BV50M', 'BV50L', 'BV6WD', 'BV6WF', 'BV6WH', 'BV6WE', 'BV7DD', 'BV6WG', 'BV7M2', 'BV7L5', 'BV7L4', 'BV71F', 'BV7M3') then 'Rebate'
	 	else 'Outras'
	 End as DsTipoTabela,

	Case
		when t1.TpPessoa = "F" and (input(SUBSTR(t1.NuCpfCnpj,6,2),18.) between 00 and 59) then "Delta 1"
		when t1.TpPessoa = "F" and (input(SUBSTR(t1.NuCpfCnpj,6,2),18.) between 60 and 79) then "Delta 2"
		when t1.TpPessoa = "F" and (input(SUBSTR(t1.NuCpfCnpj,6,2),18.) between 80 and 99) then "Delta 3"
	End as GrupoDeltas,

	Case
		when t1.NuScoreFim between 0.00 and 5.25 then 5.25
		when t1.NuScoreFim between 5.26 and 5.50 then 5.50
		when t1.NuScoreFim between 5.51 and 6.50 then 6.50
		when t1.NuScoreFim between 6.51 and 7.00 then 7.00
		when t1.NuScoreFim between 7.01 and 7.50 then 7.50
		when t1.NuScoreFim between 7.51 and 8.00 then 8.00
		when t1.NuScoreFim >= 8.01 then 8.50
	End as NuScoreFimAj,

	Case
		when t1.cdparceirocomercial in (418254, 378215) then "MFS"
    	Else "CS"
	End as CanalComercial

from HOML.tbproducao as t1
left join AUXL.tbinfocupom as t3 on (t3.NuProposta = t1.NuProposta)
inner join WORK.base_solar_originacao as t4 on (t1.NuProposta = t4.NuProposta)

left join tuyra.solar_rebate_0424 as a on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=a.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=a.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_0624 as b on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=b.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=b.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_1024 as c on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=c.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=c.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_&Data_Rebate as d on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=d.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=d.PRAZO)

where t1.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('Solar') and t4.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim

;quit;



PROC SORT DATA=base_solar NODUPKEY;
    BY NuProposta;
RUN;


proc sql;

	create table dados_medios as

		select DISTINCT
			a.nmgrupoprodutometa as Produto
			,a.CanalComercial
			,a.GrupoDeltas
			,NuScoreFimAj

			,sum(a.VrProducao) as Producao
			,sum(a.VrProducao)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as Ticket
			,sum(a.VrComissaoTotal+a.VrComissaoParcelada)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcComissao

			,sum(a.VrPerdaEstimada)/sum(a.VrFinanciamento) as RUPE

			,sum(a.VrTarifaCadastro)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrTc
			,sum(a.VpTxNet)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTxNet
			,sum(a.VpTxPactuada)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTxPac
			,sum(a.VpTirContabil)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTir
			,round(sum(a.VpPrazo)/sum(a.VrProducao),1) as Prazo
			,sum(a.VrSeguros)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrSeguros
			,sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as QntContratos
			,round(sum(a.VrCarencia)/sum(a.VrProducao),1) as CARENCIA

		from work.base_solar as a

	group by 1,2,3,4

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: ElasticidadeSep s/ cupom */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 026 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: ElasticidadeSep Nós */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 027 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 62 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: ElasticidadeSep Nós */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-B8xo5bjfiADkc1r5/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%LET Data_Simul = '06Sep2025'd;
%LET Data_Simul_Fim = '06Oct2025'd;
%LET Data_Rebate = 0925;

PROC SQL;

   CREATE TABLE TbFunilSimul_Lista_Propostas AS
   SELECT

	t1.NuAnoMesSimu,
	t1.NuProposta

	FROM Bases.TBFUNILSIMULACAO_SOLAR as t1
	left join HOML.TBORIGINACAO as t2 on (t1.NuProposta=t2.NuProposta)

	WHERE t1.DtSimulacaoProposta between &Data_Simul and &Data_Simul_Fim and t1.NuProposta is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada = 1
	and t1.CdParceiroComercial in 	and t2.DtEntradaProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim

;QUIT;





proc sql;

Create table base_solar_originacao as
Select distinct
t1.*,

	

Case
    
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then a.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then b.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then c.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then d.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
	when (t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then t1.VpTxGerencialRebate
    else .
End as VpTxNet_Orig,

Case
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then a.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then b.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then c.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then d.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then t1.VpTxPactuada
    else .
End as VpTxPactuada_Orig,

Case
    when (t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then t1.VpTirContabil
    else .
End as VpTirContabil_Orig,



Case
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then a.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then b.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then c.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then d.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then t1.VpTxGerencialRebate
    else .
End as VpTxNet_Aprov,

Case
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then a.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then b.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then c.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then d.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then t1.VpTxPactuada
    else .
End as VpTxPactuada_Aprov,

Case
    when (t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then t1.VpTirContabil
    else .
End as VpTirContabil_Aprov,



Case
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then a.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then b.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then c.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then d.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then t1.VpTxGerencialRebate
    else .
End as VpTxNet_Efet,

Case
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then a.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then b.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then c.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then d.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then t1.VpTxPactuada
    else .
End as VpTxPactuada_Efet,

Case
    when (t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then t1.VpTirContabil
    else .
End as VpTirContabil_Efet,


Case
	when t3.NmCampanhaCupom = "" THEN "-"
	else t3.NmCampanhaCupom
End as NmCampanhaCupom,

Case
	when t3.NmCampanhaCupom = "" then "N"
	else "S"
End as Flag_Cupom,



Case
    when (t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then t1.VpPrazo
    else .
End as VpPrazo_Efet

from HOML.TBORIGINACAO as t1
inner join Work.TbFunilSimul_Lista_Propostas as t2 on (t2.NuProposta = t1.NuProposta)
left join AUXL.tbinfocupom as t3 on (t3.NuProposta = t1.NuProposta)

left join tuyra.solar_rebate_0424 as a on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=a.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=a.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_0624 as b on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=b.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=b.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_1024 as c on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=c.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=c.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_&Data_Rebate as d on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=d.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=d.PRAZO)

where t1.DtEntradaProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim

;quit;



PROC SORT DATA=base_solar_originacao NODUPKEY;
    BY NuProposta;
RUN;

PROC SQL;

   CREATE TABLE ListaCPFsCupom AS
   SELECT

	t1.NuCpfCnpj

	FROM Work.base_solar_originacao as t1

	WHERE t1.Flag_Cupom = "S"
	order by 1

;QUIT;





PROC SQL;

   CREATE TABLE TbFunilSimul_Filtros AS
   SELECT

	t1.*,
	t2.DtEntradaProposta,
	t2.DtStatusProposta

	FROM Bases.TBFUNILSIMULACAO_SOLAR as t1
	left join Work.base_solar_originacao as t2 on (t1.NuProposta=t2.NuProposta)

	WHERE t1.DtSimulacaoProposta between &Data_Simul and &Data_Simul_Fim

	and t2.DtEntradaProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim
	and t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu not in (select NuCpfCnpj from work.ListaCPFsCupom)
	order by 1

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;

   CREATE TABLE Central_TbFunilSimul_TEP AS
   SELECT

	Case
		when t1.cdparceirocomercial in (418254, 378215) then "MFS"
    	Else "CS"
	End as CanalComercial,

	t1.TpPessoa,

	CASE
	    WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'F' and t1.VrSimulacao BETWEEN 5000 AND 18444 THEN '5.000 – 18.444'
	    WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'F' and t1.VrSimulacao BETWEEN 18445 AND 32374 THEN '18.445 – 32.374'
	    WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'F' and t1.VrSimulacao BETWEEN 32375 AND 54407 THEN '32.375 – 54.407'
	    WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'F' and t1.VrSimulacao BETWEEN 54408 AND 106374 THEN '54.408 – 106.374'
	    WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'F' and t1.VrSimulacao BETWEEN 106375 AND 308000 THEN '106.375 – 308.000'
		WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'F' and t1.VrSimulacao > 308000 THEN 'Acima de 308.000'
	    ELSE 'Nao PF'
	END AS FxTicketPF,

	CASE
	    WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'J' and t1.VrSimulacao BETWEEN 5000 AND 46520 THEN '5.000 – 46.520'
	    WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'J' and t1.VrSimulacao BETWEEN 46521 AND 85810 THEN '46.521 – 85.810'
	    WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'J' and t1.VrSimulacao BETWEEN 85811 AND 134623 THEN '85.811 – 134.623'
	    WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'J' and t1.VrSimulacao BETWEEN 134624 AND 206000 THEN '134.624 – 206.000'
	    WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'J' and t1.VrSimulacao BETWEEN 206001 AND 320000 THEN '206.001 – 320.000'
		WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'J' and t1.VrSimulacao > 320000 THEN 'Acima de 320.000'
	    ELSE 'Nao PJ'
	END AS FxTicketPJ,

	Case
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVXGK','BV7YG') then 'CS_Padrão'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV13J','BV7YH') then 'CS_Top 1'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV4IT','BV7YI') then 'CS_Top 2'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVXGQ','BV7Y1') then 'MFS_Padrão'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVYB3') then 'MFS_Combate I'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVYB4') then 'MFS_Combate II'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV29Y','BV7YJ') then 'MFS_Top'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVXVD','BV7YW') then 'MFS_Contingencia'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7YZ','BVXGO') then 'Comissionamento 1'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7YY','BVXGP') then 'Comissionamento 2'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7II') then 'Feirao'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7LV','BV7YX') then 'Guerrilha Padrão'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV60U', 'BV7DH', 'BV7LH') then 'Belenus'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7LX') then 'Fotus'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7U5') then 'ASC'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7O3') then 'SolarPrime'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV654') then 'AVT'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7FL') then 'EcoPower'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7YB') then 'Guerrilha Sul'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV2B5','BV653','BV6L3') then 'Especiais'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in  ('BV600', 'BV679', 'BV670', 'BV68A', 'BV68B', 'BV68C', 'BV7D5', 'BV7EX', 'BV50M', 'BV50L', 'BV6WD', 'BV6WF', 'BV6WH', 'BV6WE', 'BV7DD', 'BV6WG', 'BV7M2', 'BV7L5', 'BV7L4', 'BV71F', 'BV7M3') then 'Rebate'
	    else 'Outras'
	End as DsTipoTabela,

	Case
		when t1.TpPessoa = "F" and t1.DtSimulacaoProposta >= '06Sep2025'd and (input(SUBSTR(t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu,6,2),18.) between 00 and 59) then "Delta 1"
		when t1.TpPessoa = "F" and t1.DtSimulacaoProposta >= '06Sep2025'd and (input(SUBSTR(t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu,6,2),18.) between 60 and 79) then "Delta 2"
		when t1.TpPessoa = "F" and t1.DtSimulacaoProposta >= '06Sep2025'd and (input(SUBSTR(t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu,6,2),18.) between 80 and 99) then "Delta 3"
	End as GrupoDeltas,

	Case
	    when t1.GhEntrada in (0, 2.5, 3, 3.5, 4.5, 4.75, 5.25) then 5.25
	    when t1.GhEntrada = 5.5 then 5.50
	    when t1.GhEntrada in (6.25, 6.5) then 6.50
	    when t1.GhEntrada = 7 then 7.00
	    when t1.GhEntrada = 7.5 then 7.50
	    when t1.GhEntrada in (7.75, 8) then 8.00
	    when t1.GhEntrada in (8.25, 8.5, 9) then 8.50
	End as NuScoreFimAj,

	COUNT (DISTINCT t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu) AS QtdSimu,
	COUNT (Distinct MSGPOS.NuCpfCNPJSimu) AS QtdSimuAprov,
	SUM (t1.FlPropostaOriginada) AS QtdOrig,
	SUM (t1.FlPropostaAprovada) AS QtdAprov,
	SUM (case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then 1 else 0 end) AS QtdEfet,

	SUM (t1.VrPropostaOriginada) AS VrOrig,
	SUM (t1.VrPropostaAprovada) AS VrAprov,
	SUM (case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end) AS VrEfet,



	(COUNT (Distinct MSGPOS.NuCpfCNPJSimu) / COUNT (DISTINCT t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu)) as MSgPositiva format=percent8.2,
	(SUM (t1.FlPropostaOriginada) / COUNT (Distinct MSGPOS.NuCpfCNPJSimu)) as Proposta format=percent8.2,
	(SUM (t1.FlPropostaAprovada) / SUM (t1.FlPropostaOriginada)) as Aprovacao format=percent8.2,
	(SUM (case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then 1 else 0 end) / SUM (t1.FlPropostaAprovada)) as Efetivacao format=percent8.2,

	(SUM (case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then 1 else 0 end) / COUNT (DISTINCT t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu)) as ConvGeral format=percent8.2,
	((COUNT (Distinct MSGPOS.NuCpfCNPJSimu) / COUNT (DISTINCT t1.NuCpfCNPJSimu)) * (SUM (t1.FlPropostaAprovada) / SUM (t1.FlPropostaOriginada))) as FatorAprovacao format=percent8.2,
	((SUM (t1.FlPropostaOriginada) / COUNT (Distinct MSGPOS.NuCpfCNPJSimu)) * (SUM (case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then 1 else 0 end) / SUM (t1.FlPropostaAprovada))) as FatorEfetivacao format=percent8.2,



	
	(SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Orig)) AS Valor_Tx_Orig,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Orig)) AS Valor_Tx_Pactuada_Orig,
	(SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Orig)) AS Valor_TIR_Orig,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada)) AS Tx_Orig,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada)) AS Tx_Pactuada_Ori,
	((1+SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada))/(1+SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada))-1) AS Spread_Net_Orig,
	((1+SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada))/(1+SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Orig)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaOriginada))-1) AS Spread_Pactuada_Orig,

	
	(SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Aprov)) AS Valor_Tx_Aprov,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Aprov)) AS Valor_Tx_Pactuada_Aprov,
	(SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Aprov)) AS Valor_TIR_Aprov,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada)) AS Tx_Aprov,
	(SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada)) AS Tx_Pactuada_Aprov,
	((1+SUM(t4.VpTxNet_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada))/(1+SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada))-1) AS Spread_Net_Aprov,
	((1+SUM(t4.VpTxPactuada_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada))/(1+SUM(t4.VpTirContabil_Aprov)/SUM(t1.VrPropostaAprovada))-1) AS Spread_Pactuada_Aprov,

	
	(SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxNet_Efet else 0 end)) AS Valor_Tx_Efet,
	(SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxPactuada_Efet else 0 end)) AS Valor_Tx_Pactuada_Efet,
	(SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTirContabil_Efet else 0 end)) AS Valor_TIR_Efet,
	(SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxNet_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end)) AS Tx_Efet,
	(SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxPactuada_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end)) AS Tx_Pactuada_Efet,
	((1+SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxNet_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end))/(1+SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTirContabil_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end))-1) AS Spread_Net_Efet,
	((1+SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTxPactuada_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end))/(1+SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VpTirContabil_Efet else 0 end)/SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end))-1) AS Spread_Pactuada_Efet,


	SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then VpPrazo_Efet else 0 end) as VpPrazo_Efet,
	SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then VpPrazo_Efet else 0 end) / SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada  else 0 end) as PrazoEfet,
	SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t1.VrPropostaEfetivada else 0 end) / SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then 1 else 0 end) as TicketEfet,
	SUM(case when t1.FlPropostaEfetivada =1 and t1.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim then t4.VrPDD else 0 end) as VrPerdaEstimada

	FROM Work.TbFunilSimul_Filtros as t1

	Left Join
	   (Select CodSimu, NuCpfCNPJSimu
	    From Bases.TbFunilSimulacao_Solar
	    Where DtSimulacaoProposta between &Data_Simul and &Data_Simul_Fim and FlMSGPositiva = 1
	    Group by 1) as MSGPOS
	On t1.CodSimu = MSGPOS.CodSimu

	Left Join WORK.base_solar_originacao as t4 on (t1.NuProposta = t4.NuProposta)

	GROUP BY 1,2,3,4,5,6,7
	order by 1

;QUIT;

proc sql;

Create table base_solar as
Select distinct
t1.*,

(t1.DtPrimeiroVencimento - t1.DtContrato)*(t1.VrProducao) as VrCarencia,

	Case
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null) then a.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null) then b.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null) then c.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null) then d.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		else t1.VpTxGerencialRebate
	End as VpTxNet,

	Case
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null) then a.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null) then b.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null) then c.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null) then d.TxRebate*t1.VrProducao
		else t1.VpTxJuros
	End as VpTxPactuada,


Case
	when t3.NmCampanhaCupom = "" THEN "-"
	else t3.NmCampanhaCupom
End as NmCampanhaCupom,

Case
	when t3.NmCampanhaCupom = "" then "N"
	else "S"
End as Flag_Cupom,

	Case
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVXGK','BV7YG') then 'CS_Padrão'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV13J','BV7YH') then 'CS_Top 1'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV4IT','BV7YI') then 'CS_Top 2'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVXGQ','BV7Y1') then 'MFS_Padrão'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVYB3') then 'MFS_Combate I'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVYB4') then 'MFS_Combate II'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV29Y','BV7YJ') then 'MFS_Top'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BVXVD','BV7YW') then 'MFS_Contingencia'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7YZ','BVXGO') then 'Comissionamento 1'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7YY','BVXGP') then 'Comissionamento 2'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7II') then 'Feirao'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7LV','BV7YX') then 'Guerrilha Padrão'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV60U', 'BV7DH', 'BV7LH') then 'Belenus'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7LX') then 'Fotus'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7U5') then 'ASC'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7O3') then 'SolarPrime'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV654') then 'AVT'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7FL') then 'EcoPower'
	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV7YB') then 'Guerrilha Sul'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in ('BV2B5','BV653','BV6L3') then 'Especiais'

	    when t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento in  ('BV600', 'BV679', 'BV670', 'BV68A', 'BV68B', 'BV68C', 'BV7D5', 'BV7EX', 'BV50M', 'BV50L', 'BV6WD', 'BV6WF', 'BV6WH', 'BV6WE', 'BV7DD', 'BV6WG', 'BV7M2', 'BV7L5', 'BV7L4', 'BV71F', 'BV7M3') then 'Rebate'
	 	else 'Outras'
	 End as DsTipoTabela,

	Case
		when t1.TpPessoa = "F" and (input(SUBSTR(t1.NuCpfCnpj,6,2),18.) between 00 and 59) then "Delta 1"
		when t1.TpPessoa = "F" and (input(SUBSTR(t1.NuCpfCnpj,6,2),18.) between 60 and 79) then "Delta 2"
		when t1.TpPessoa = "F" and (input(SUBSTR(t1.NuCpfCnpj,6,2),18.) between 80 and 99) then "Delta 3"
	End as GrupoDeltas,

	Case
		when t1.NuScoreFim between 0.00 and 5.25 then 5.25
		when t1.NuScoreFim between 5.26 and 5.50 then 5.50
		when t1.NuScoreFim between 5.51 and 6.50 then 6.50
		when t1.NuScoreFim between 6.51 and 7.00 then 7.00
		when t1.NuScoreFim between 7.01 and 7.50 then 7.50
		when t1.NuScoreFim between 7.51 and 8.00 then 8.00
		when t1.NuScoreFim >= 8.01 then 8.50
	End as NuScoreFimAj,

	Case
		when t1.cdparceirocomercial in (418254, 378215) then "MFS"
    	Else "CS"
	End as CanalComercial,

		CASE
	    WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'F' and t1.VrProducao BETWEEN 5000 AND 18444 THEN '5.000 – 18.444'
	    WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'F' and t1.VrProducao BETWEEN 18445 AND 32374 THEN '18.445 – 32.374'
	    WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'F' and t1.VrProducao BETWEEN 32375 AND 54407 THEN '32.375 – 54.407'
	    WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'F' and t1.VrProducao BETWEEN 54408 AND 106374 THEN '54.408 – 106.374'
	    WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'F' and t1.VrProducao BETWEEN 106375 AND 308000 THEN '106.375 – 308.000'
		WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'F' and t1.VrProducao > 308000 THEN 'Acima de 308.000'
	    ELSE 'Nao PF'
	END AS FxTicketPF,

	CASE
	    WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'J' and t1.VrProducao BETWEEN 5000 AND 46520 THEN '5.000 – 46.520'
	    WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'J' and t1.VrProducao BETWEEN 46521 AND 85810 THEN '46.521 – 85.810'
	    WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'J' and t1.VrProducao BETWEEN 85811 AND 134623 THEN '85.811 – 134.623'
	    WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'J' and t1.VrProducao BETWEEN 134624 AND 206000 THEN '134.624 – 206.000'
	    WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'J' and t1.VrProducao BETWEEN 206001 AND 320000 THEN '206.001 – 320.000'
		WHEN t1.Tppessoa = 'J' and t1.VrProducao > 320000 THEN 'Acima de 320.000'
	    ELSE 'Nao PJ'
	END AS FxTicketPJ

from HOML.tbproducao as t1
left join AUXL.tbinfocupom as t3 on (t3.NuProposta = t1.NuProposta)
inner join WORK.base_solar_originacao as t4 on (t1.NuProposta = t4.NuProposta)

left join tuyra.solar_rebate_0424 as a on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=a.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=a.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_0624 as b on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=b.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=b.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_1024 as c on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=c.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=c.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_&Data_Rebate as d on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=d.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=d.PRAZO)

where t1.nmgrupoprodutometa in ('Solar') and t4.DtStatusProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim

;quit;



PROC SORT DATA=base_solar NODUPKEY;
    BY NuProposta;
RUN;


proc sql;

	create table dados_medios as

		select DISTINCT
			a.nmgrupoprodutometa as Produto,
			a.CanalComercial,
			a.TpPessoa,
			a.FxTicketPF,
			a.FxTicketPJ,
			a.DsTipoTabela,
			a.GrupoDeltas,
			a.NuScoreFimAj,

			sum(a.VrProducao) as Producao,
			sum(a.VrProducao)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as Ticket,
			sum(a.VrComissaoTotal+a.VrComissaoParcelada)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcComissao,

			sum(a.VrPerdaEstimada)/sum(a.VrFinanciamento) as RUPE,

			sum(a.VrTarifaCadastro)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrTc,
			sum(a.VpTxNet)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTxNet,
			sum(a.VpTxPactuada)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTxPac,
			sum(a.VpTirContabil)/sum(a.VrProducao) as PcTir,
			round(sum(a.VpPrazo)/sum(a.VrProducao),1) as Prazo,
			sum(a.VrSeguros)/sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as VrSeguros,
			sum(case when (a.NuContrato) > 0 then 1 else 0 end) as QntContratos,
			round(sum(a.VrCarencia)/sum(a.VrProducao),1) as CARENCIA

		from work.base_solar as a

	group by 1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8

	;
	quit;

%LET Data_Rebate = 0925;

PROC SQL;

(415552,415893,425370,444588,450776,453385,461801,462608,463040,463065,464332,464931,465693,466770,410598,413976,469425,469492,469670,469981,470116,470153,470175,470226,470249,470267,470333,470359,470410,470491,470522,470542,470602,470654,471004,471341,471430,471452,471495,471654,471666,471691,471702,471708,471732,471734,471920,472060,472062,472081,472102,472184,472312,473597,474096,474277,474321,474467,474973,475517,397370,398989,399020,399200,399278,399511,399535,399573,399580,399593,399899,400013,400014,400122,400123,400439,400558,400607,400655,400705,400805,400871,400942,400967,401044,401147,401445,401588,401625,401754,402330,402424,402426,402521,402837,402882,402898,402911,402991,403024,403370,403373,403390,403398,403467,403587,403731,404091,404094,404135,404139,404434,404755,404937,405417,405976,406029,408890,410213,410352,410598,410645,410794,410878,411124,411303,411395,411476,411488,411614,411617,411646,411679,411681,411683,411809,411822,411824,411849,411952,411996,412007,412096,412161,412164,412190,412252,412377,412485,412503,412515,412531,412561,412615,412684,412713,412739,412791,412795,412937,413239,413241,413252,413270,413427,413432,413467,413506,413510,413531,413577,413634,413639,413644,413779,413792,413849,413964,413976,414035,414037,414038,414077,414081,414095,414100,414102,414139,414141,414173,414195,414274,414335,414350,414356,414404,414408,414440,414452,414458,414478,414488,414532,414567,414595,414626,414628,414679,414684,414722,414748,414830,414834,414873,414912,414919,414958,415101,415144,415175,415185,415224,415305,415322,415324,415330,415343,415364,415374,415394,415552,415663,415685,415688,415721,415729,415772,415779,415804,415895,415932,415946,415959,416071,416083,416277,416339,416350,416585,416718,416742,416851,417350,417359,417746,417783,417845,417905,417954,418197,418200,418213,418229,418238,418254,418259,418270,418392,418752,418917,418961,419141,419149,419331,419447,419488,419502,419664,419791,419813,420109,420111,420117,420205,420255,4203 	and t2.DtEntradaProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim

;QUIT;





proc sql;

Create table base_solar_originacao as
Select distinct
t1.*,

	

Case
    
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then a.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then b.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then c.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then d.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
	when (t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then t1.VpTxGerencialRebate
    else .
End as VpTxNet_Orig,

Case
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then a.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then b.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then c.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then d.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaOriginada
    when (t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then t1.VpTxPactuada
    else .
End as VpTxPactuada_Orig,

Case
    when (t1.FlPropostaOriginada > 0) then t1.VpTirContabil
    else .
End as VpTirContabil_Orig,



Case
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then a.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then b.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then c.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then d.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then t1.VpTxGerencialRebate
    else .
End as VpTxNet_Aprov,

Case
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then a.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then b.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then c.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then d.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaAprovada
    when (t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then t1.VpTxPactuada
    else .
End as VpTxPactuada_Aprov,

Case
    when (t1.FlPropostaAprovada > 0) then t1.VpTirContabil
    else .
End as VpTirContabil_Aprov,



Case
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then a.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then b.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then c.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then d.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then t1.VpTxGerencialRebate
    else .
End as VpTxNet_Efet,

Case
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta < '03jun2024'd and a.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then a.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '03jun2024'd and b.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then b.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01jul2024'd and c.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then c.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.DtEntradaProposta >= '01nov2024'd and d.TABELA is not null and t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then d.TxRebate * t1.VrPropostaEfetivada
    when (t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then t1.VpTxPactuada
    else .
End as VpTxPactuada_Efet,

Case
    when (t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then t1.VpTirContabil
    else .
End as VpTirContabil_Efet,


Case
	when t3.NmCampanhaCupom = "" THEN "-"
	else t3.NmCampanhaCupom
End as NmCampanhaCupom,

Case
	when t3.NmCampanhaCupom = "" then "N"
	else "S"
End as Flag_Cupom,



Case
    when (t1.FlPropostaEfetivada > 0) then t1.VpPrazo
    else .
End as VpPrazo_Efet

from HOML.TBORIGINACAO as t1
inner join Work.TbFunilSimul_Lista_Propostas as t2 on (t2.NuProposta = t1.NuProposta)
left join AUXL.tbinfocupom as t3 on (t3.NuProposta = t1.NuProposta)

left join tuyra.solar_rebate_0424 as a on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=a.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=a.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_0624 as b on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=b.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=b.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_1024 as c on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=c.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=c.PRAZO)
left join tuyra.solar_rebate_&Data_Rebate as d on (t1.CdTabelaFinanciamento=d.TABELA and t1.QtParcelas=d.PRAZO)

where t1.DtEntradaProposta <= &Data_Simul_Fim

;quit;



PROC SORT DATA=base_solar_originacao NODUPKEY;
    BY NuProposta;
RUN;

PROC SQL;

   CREATE TABLE ListaCPFsCupom AS
   SELECT

	t1.NuCpfCnpj

	FROM Work.base_solar_originacao as t1

	WHERE t1.Flag_Cupom = "S"
	order by 1

;QUIT;





PROC SQL;
